 {"content": "# Sports Federation Discipline\n\nIncident reporting, disciplinary case management, sanctions, and player\nsuspensions. Provides the full disciplinary pipeline from incident detection\nthrough case resolution to sanction enforcement.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTracks **incidents** that occur during matches, groups them into **disciplinary\ncases** for investigation, and records **sanctions** (fines, warnings) and\n**suspensions** (match bans) as outcomes. Links back to players, clubs, and\nreferees for comprehensive disciplinary records.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n| Module | Reason |\n|--------|--------|\n| `sports_federation_base` | Clubs |\n| `sports_federation_people` | Players |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Matches |\n| `mail` | Chatter |\n\n## Models\n\n### `federation.match.incident`\n\nAn event that occurred during a match that may trigger disciplinary action.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Incident title |\n| `match_id` | Many2one | The match |\n| `player_id` | Many2one | Involved player |\n| `club_id` | Many2one | Involved club |\n| `referee_id` | Many2one | Reporting referee |\n| `incident_type` | Selection | warning / yellow_card / red_card / misconduct / violence / admin_issue / other |\n| `minute_text` | Char | Match minute (text for flexibility) |\n| `description` | Text | What happened |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Linked disciplinary case |\n| `date_reported` | Date | When reported |\n| `reported_by_user_id` | Many2one | System user who filed it |\n| `status` | Selection | new / attached / closed |\n\n### `federation.disciplinary.case`\n\nAn investigation triggered by one or more incidents.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Case title |\n| `reference` | Char | Auto-sequence reference number |\n| `state` | Selection | draft / under_review / decided / appealed / closed |\n| `opened_on` / `decided_on` / `closed_on` | Date | Timeline |\n| `responsible_user_id` | Many2one | Case handler |\n| `incident_ids` | One2many | Related incidents |\n| `sanction_ids` | One2many | Resulting sanctions |\n| `suspension_ids` | One2many | Resulting suspensions |\n| `incident_count` / `sanction_count` / `suspension_count` | Integer | Dedicated stat counters for case shortcuts |\n| `subject_player_id` / `subject_club_id` / `subject_referee_id` | Many2one | Subject |\n| `summary` / `notes` | Text | Case details |\n\n- **State machine**: draft → under_review → decided → appealed / closed, with under-review cases allowed back to draft for corrections before a decision is recorded.\n- **Auto-numbering** via `ir.sequence`.\n- **Counter labels** use distinct technical field names so the case relation tabs and their stat counters do not trigger duplicate-label warnings during module loading.\n\n### `federation.sanction`\n\nA penalty imposed as the outcome of a disciplinary case.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Sanction title |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Parent case |\n| `sanction_type` | Selection | fine / warning / ban / point_deduction / other |\n| ValidationError occurs.\"\"\"\n        self._create_roster_workspace_smoke_data()\n        self.authenticate(self.season_user.login, \"ignored\")\n\n        # Hit the roster list page with an error_hint query param (simulating a\n        # redirect from a failed POST) and verify the hint renders.\n        hint_text = \"Ensure the registration is confirmed and a roster has not already been  {"content": "# Sports Federation Scheduling\n\nThe scheduling addon owns working schedules, fixture assignments, schedule\nchanges, and the weighted fairness auto-schedule preview.\n\nThe auto-schedule wizard previews deterministic assignments after applying hard\nconstraints and weighted fairness penalties. Applying a proposal records the\nautomatic assignments and schedule changes while checking the schedule revision\nfor concurrent edits.\n\nAll access-control rows, including the auto-schedule wizard permissions, are\nloaded through `security/ir.model.access.csv`.\n\n\n## Operator handoff to independent review\n\nA planner completes fixture-to-slot assignments in **Schedule Planner** and uses\n**Submit for Review**. Blocking validation errors prevent submission. When only\nwarnings remain, a confirmation dialog requires an explicit justification. The\nbutton delegates to `federation.schedule.commands`; when Schedule Approval is\ninstalled, the approval extension creates the immutable pending review in the\nsame transaction and opens it directly.\n\nSubmitted schedules are immutable. If a reviewer requests changes, the schedule\nreturns to `changes_requested`, where the planner can edit and submit a new\nrevision. Planners can use the **Reviews** smart button to inspect the current\nhandoff and retained history.\n", "content_length": 1297, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_scheduling/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "5739035035f9d4bd235fa1da1e9642c79bb0ee7d94ffcf3a5f662c6e2bc211b7", "size_bytes": 1297}

 {"content": "from odoo import api, models\nfrom odoo.exceptions import ValidationError\n\n\nclass FederationScheduleIntegrity(models.Model):\n    _inherit = \"federation.schedule\"\n\n    _MUTABLE_STATES = (\"draft\", \"changes_requested\")\n    _PROTECTED_FIELDS = {\"edition_id\", \"structure_id\", \"matchday_id\", \"assignment_ids\"}\n\n    @api.constrains(\"matchday_id\", \"state\")\n    def _check_one_active_schedule(self):\n        for record in self.filtered(lambda item: item.state != \"superseded\"):\n            duplicate = self.search_count(\n                [\n                    (\"matchday_id\", \"=\", record.matchday_id.id),\n                    (\"state\", \"!=\", \"superseded\"),\n                    (\"id\", \"!=\", record.id),\n                ]\n            )\n            if duplicate:\n                raise ValidationError(\n                    \"A match day can have only one active working schedule.\"\n                )\n\n    def action_create_revision(self, reason):\n        self.ensure_one()\n        if self.state != \"published\":\n            raise ValidationError(\n                \"Only a published schedule can start a replacement revision.\"\n            )\n        if self.matchday_id.state == \"open\":\n            raise ValidationError(\n                \"Close live match-day operations before replacing its schedule.\"\n            )\n        if not (reason or \"\").strip():\n            raise ValidationError(\n                \"Explain why the published schedule must be replaced.\"\n            )\n        self.env[\"federation.competition.role.assignment\"].assert_role(\n            self.edition_id, \"schedule_planner\", \"competition_director\"\n        )\n        self.env.cr.execute(\n            \"SELECT id FROM federation_schedule WHERE id = %s FOR UPDATE\",\n            [self.id],\n        )\n        self.invalidate_recordset([\"state\", \"superseded_by_id\"])\n        if self.state != \"published\":\n            raise ValidationError(\n                _(\"Only the current published schedule can be amended.\")\n            )\n        if self.superseded_by_id:\n            raise ValidationError(\n                _(\"A replacement revision already exists for this schedule.\")\n            )\n        self.state = \"superseded\"\n        replacement = self.copy(\n            {\n                \"name\": f\"{self.name} revision\",\n                \"state\": \"changes_requested\",\n                \"revision\": 0,\n                \"supersedes_id\": self.id,\n                \"revision_reason\": reason,\n                \"change_ids\": False,\n            }\n        )\n        if self.assignment_ids:\n            self.env[\"federation.schedule.assignment\"].create(\n                [\n                    {\n                        \"schedule_id\": replacement.id,\n                        \"fixture_id\": assignment.fixture_id.id,\n                        \"slot_id\": assignment.slot_id.id,\n                        \"method\": assignment.method,\n                        \"assigned_by_id\": assignment.assigned_by_id.id,\n                    }\n                    for assignment in self.assignment_ids\n                ]\n            )\n        self.superseded_by_id = replacement.id\n        return replacement\n\n    def assert_mutable(self):\n        if self.filtered(lambda record: record.state not in self._MUTABLE_STATES):\n            raise ValidationError(\n                \"Submitted, approved and published schedules are immutable.\"\n            )\n        return True\n\n    def write(self, vals):\n        if self._PROTECTED_FIELDS.intersection(vals):\n            self.assert_mutable()\n        return super().write(vals)\n\n    def unlink(self):\n        self.assert_mutable()\n        return super().unlink()\n\n\nclass FederationScheduleAssignmentIntegrity(models.Model):\n    _inherit = \"federation.schedule.assignment\"\n\n    @api.model_create_multi\n    def create(self, vals_list):\n        self.env[\"federation.schedule\"].browse(\n            [vals[\"schedule_id\"] for vals in vals_list if vals.get(\"schedule_id\")]\n        ).assert_mutable()\n        return super().create(vals_list)\n\n    def write(self, vals):\n        self.mapped(\"schedule_id\").assert_mutable()\n        return super().write(vals)\n\n    def unlink(self):\n        self.mapped(\"schedule_id\").assert_mutable()\n        return super().unlink()\n", "content_length": 4195, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state":
